CR 24 (XP 62,000; PB +7)

Traits

Amphibious. The song leviathan can breathe air and water.

Sound the Hull. A creature that hits the song leviathan with a melee attack while within 5 feet of it takes 14 (4d6) Thunder damage and has the Deafened condition until the end of its next turn.

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the song leviathan fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.

Actions

Multiattack. The song leviathan makes two Crushing Limb attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Creeping Dread.

Crushing Limb. Melee Attack Roll: +16, reach 10 ft. 22 (3d8 + 9) Bludgeoning damage plus 14 (4d6) Thunder damage.

Drowning Hymn (Recharge 5-6). Constitution Saving Throw: DC 24, each creature in a 60-foot Cone. Failure: 78 (12d12) Thunder damage, and the target has the Deafened condition until the end of its next turn. Success: Half damage only.

Creeping Dread. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 24, one creature within 60 feet. Failure: The target has the Frightened condition until the end of the song leviathan's next turn. If the target was already Frightened by this ability, it also takes 38 (11d6) Thunder damage.

Legendary Actions

Legendary Action Uses: 3. Immediately after another creature's turn, the song leviathan can expend a use to take one of the following actions. It regains all expended uses at the start of each of its turns.

Lunge and Crush. The song leviathan moves up to half its Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks, and it makes one Crushing Limb attack.

Trench Slam. Strength Saving Throw: DC 24, each creature within 10 feet of the song leviathan. Failure: The target has the Prone condition.

[concept] identity: The leviathan opens with its drowning song, a subsonic hymn that rolls through the water and staggers every hull and swimmer within a mile, then lunges in to crush the paralyzed with limbs of black trench-chitin and a maw wide enough to take a ship's bow. Each round it keeps singing while it strikes, layering Thunder through every slam so the fleet's crews drown in sound before they drown in brine, and it fights to the death — a thing of the abyss that has never learned retreat. It toys only at the edges of the song, letting survivors drift half-conscious before dragging them under.
[concept] lore: It has sung in the lightless trenches since before the coastal kingdoms charted their first sea lane, and it surfaces only to answer the noise of keels and engines with a song that pulls whole fleets down to its nursery of bones.

Faultless Tracker. The silt hound knows the direction and distance to any creature it has damaged within the last 24 hours, as long as the two of them are on the same plane of existence.
Whelm. As a bonus action, the silt hound whelms one Medium or smaller creature it is grappling. While whelmed, the target is blinded and restrained, has total cover against attacks and other effects outside the silt hound, and takes 3 (1d6) bludgeoning damage at the start of each of the silt hound's turns. The silt hound can have only one creature whelmed at a time. If the silt hound takes 5 damage or more in a single turn from the whelmed creature, the silt hound must release the target at the end of that turn, and the target exits prone in an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the silt hound. If the silt hound dies, a whelmed creature pulls free prone by using 5 feet of movement.

Actions
Multiattack: The silt hound makes two Bite attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Mire Drag.
Bite: Melee Weapon Attack: +3 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 5 (1d8 + 1) piercing damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it is grappled (escape DC 11). While grappled, the target is restrained.
Mire Drag: One creature within 20 feet of the silt hound must succeed on a DC 13 Strength saving throw or be pulled up to 15 feet straight toward the silt hound by its mud-caked bulk and be grappled (escape DC 11).
```

Senses darkvision 60 ft.; Passive Perception 13

Languages Common plus one other language

CR 3 (XP 700; PB +2)

Traits

Ethereal Sight. The bellwraith can see 60 feet into the Ethereal Plane when it is on the Material Plane.

Incorporeal Movement. The bellwraith can move through other creatures and objects as if they were Difficult Terrain. It takes 5 (1d10) Force damage if it ends its turn inside an object.

Actions

Multiattack. The bellwraith makes two Withering Touch attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Horrific Visage.

Withering Touch. Melee Attack Roll: +5, reach 5 ft. 19 (3d10 + 3) Necrotic damage.

Horrific Visage. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 13, each creature in a 60-foot Cone that can see the bellwraith and isn't an Undead. Failure: 10 (2d6 + 3) Psychic damage, and the target has the Frightened condition until the start of the bellwraith's next turn. Success: The target is immune to this ghost's Horrific Visage for 24 hours.

Possession (Recharge 6). Charisma Saving Throw: DC 13, one Humanoid the bellwraith can see within 5 feet. Failure: The target is possessed by the bellwraith; the bellwraith disappears, and the target has the Incapacitated condition and loses control of its body. The bellwraith now controls the body, but the target retains awareness. The bellwraith can't be targeted by any attack, spell, or other effect, except ones that specifically target Undead. The bellwraith's game statistics are the same, except it uses the possessed target's Speed, as well as the target's Strength, Dexterity, and Constitution modifiers.
The possession lasts until the body drops to 0 Hit Points or the bellwraith leaves as a Bonus Action. When the possession ends, the bellwraith appears in an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the target, and the target is immune to this ghost's Possession for 24 hours. Success: The target is immune to this ghost's Possession for 24 hours.

Etherealness. The bellwraith casts the Etherealness spell, requiring no spell components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability. The bellwraith is visible on the Material Plane while on the Border Ethereal and vice versa, but it can't affect or be affected by anything on the other plane.

Entomb. As a bonus action, the pit entombs one Medium or smaller creature it is grappling. While entombed, the target is blinded and restrained, has total cover against attacks and other effects outside the pit, and takes 7 (2d6) bludgeoning damage at the start of each of the pit's turns. The pit can have only one creature entombed at a time. If the pit takes 15 damage or more in a single turn from the entombed creature, the pit must release the target at the end of that turn, and the target exits prone in an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the pit. If the pit dies, an entombed creature breaks free prone by using 5 feet of movement.

Actions
Multiattack: The pit makes two attacks: one with its Slam and one with its Swallow Into the Earth.
Slam: Melee Weapon Attack: +6 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 16 (3d8 + 3) bludgeoning damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it is grappled (escape DC 14). While grappled, the target is restrained.
Earthburst Strike (Recharge 5-6): If the pit is burrowing, it erupts from the ground at a point within 5 feet of one creature. That creature must make a DC 14 Dexterity saving throw, taking 28 (8d6) bludgeoning damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one. On a failed save, the target is also knocked prone. The pit then makes one Slam attack against the target.
Swallow Into the Earth: Melee Weapon Attack: +6 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 16 (3d8 + 3) bludgeoning damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it is grappled (escape DC 14).

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the red dragon fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.

Actions
Multiattack: The red dragon makes three Rend attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Spellcasting to cast Scorching Ray.
Rend: Melee Weapon Attack: +14 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 13 (1d10 + 8) slashing damage plus 5 (2d4) fire damage.
Spellcasting: The red dragon casts one of the following spells, requiring no material components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 20, +12 to hit with spell attacks): -

At will: command, detect magic, scorching ray

1/day each: fireball
Fire Breath (Recharge 5-6): Each creature in a 60-foot cone must make a DC 21 Dexterity saving throw, taking 59 (17d6) fire damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one.
Legendary Actions

The red dragon can take 3 legendary actions, choosing from the options below. Only one legendary action option can be used at a time and only at the end of another creature's turn. The red dragon regains spent legendary actions at the start of its turn.

Commanding Presence: The red dragon uses Spellcasting to cast command (cast at 2nd level). The red dragon can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
Fiery Rays: The red dragon uses Spellcasting to cast scorching ray. The red dragon can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
Pounce: The red dragon moves up to half its speed, and it makes one Rend attack.

[concept] identity: An Adult Red Dragon that lost one eye to a lance long ago, and it opens every fight with a strafing breath pass that favors its blind side — a dead angle it deliberately presents so prey commits, then punishes with a tail sweep and bite as it banks around. Round to round it circles the battlefield at height, keeping that scarred cheek toward the thickest cluster of foes, then drops into a crushing full-body landing where its blind-side arc is the deadliest and least predictable attack vector. Wounded and cornered, it does not flee — it fights with a bitter, calculating fury, using its ruined eye as bait and turtling into armoured coils when enemies press the blind flank.
[concept] lore: This red wyrm was lamed in a duel with a mounted lancer who drove a spear through its eye before dying underfoot; it has kept the broken lance-head lodged in the socket ever since and now prowls the cinder-ridges hunting the knight's descendants and any who carry that sigil.

Traits

Molten Seams. A creature that touches the tyrant or hits it with a melee attack while within 5 feet of it takes 10 (3d6) Fire damage.

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the tyrant fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.

Aura of Authority. While in a 10-foot Emanation originating from the tyrant, the tyrant and its allies have Advantage on attack rolls and saving throws, provided the tyrant doesn't have the Incapacitated condition.

Actions

Multiattack. The tyrant makes two Fist attacks.

Fist. Melee Attack Roll: +12, reach 10 ft. 15 (2d8 + 6) Bludgeoning damage plus 13 (3d8) Fire damage.

Bonus Actions

Molten Retort. One creature within 5 feet that hit the tyrant with an attack roll since the end of the tyrant's last turn takes 13 (3d8) Fire damage.

Legendary Actions

Legendary Action Uses: 3. Immediately after another creature's turn, the tyrant can expend a use to take one of the following actions. It regains all expended uses at the start of each of its turns.

Anvil Advance. The tyrant moves up to half its Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks, and it makes one Fist attack.

Rallying Command. One ally of the tyrant that can see or hear it can immediately use its Reaction to move up to half its Speed or to make one attack.

Quench Ranks. The tyrant and each of its allies within 10 feet of it that can see or hear it end the Charmed and Frightened conditions on themselves.

[concept] identity: A stone giant exile who was cast out of its clan, it wades into battle swinging a slab-sized fist of raw granite and simply outlasts whatever stands in front of it, planting its feet and trading blows like a landslide given a grudge. It does not flee when bloodied; the exile has nowhere left to go, so it fights to the last, and when badly wounded its stone hide cracks and it only swings harder, treating any dying ground as a better grave than the one it was driven from.
[concept] lore: Driven from its mountain clan for some dishonor it still declares its innocence of only in Giant, this exile wanders the lowlands swinging at anything it deems a challenger, wanting nothing more than to be proven right by force before it is proven right by kin.

Rider's Evasion. Krennik ignores difficult terrain, and opportunity attacks against it are made with disadvantage.
Mounted Agility. As a bonus action, Krennik takes the Dash or Disengage action.

Actions
Multiattack: Krennik makes two attacks: one with War Lance and one with Standard Cast.
War Lance: Melee Weapon Attack: +5 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 10 (2d6 + 3) piercing damage. If Krennik moved at least 20 feet in a straight line toward the target immediately before the hit, the target takes an extra 4 (1d8) piercing damage and, if it is Medium or smaller, is knocked prone.
Galloping Charge (Recharge 5-6): Krennik moves up to its speed without provoking opportunity attacks and can move through the spaces of Medium or smaller creatures. Each creature whose space it enters is targeted once by the following effect. Each such creature must make a DC 13 Strength saving throw, taking 22 (5d8) bludgeoning damage on a failed save, and the target is knocked prone.
Standard Cast: Krennik hurls a hurled object. Ranged Weapon Attack: +5 to hit, range 30/120 ft., one target. Hit: 10 (2d6 + 3) bludgeoning damage. The weapon lands in an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the target; until Krennik spends 5 feet of movement in that space to recover it, Krennik's speed is halved and it can't use this action.

CR 4 (XP 1,100; PB +2)

Traits

Amphibious. The sorrowlarch can breathe air and water.

Spider-Jointed Frame. The sorrowlarch ignores difficult terrain and can climb sheer surfaces, including chimneys, using its full Speed.

False Appearance. While the sorrowlarch remains motionless, it is indistinguishable from an ordinary object.

Actions

Multiattack. The sorrowlarch makes two attacks, using Fists or Hooked Limbs in any combination.

Fists. Melee Attack Roll: +6, reach 5 ft. 13 (2d8 + 4) Bludgeoning damage.

Hooked Limbs. Melee Attack Roll: +6, reach 5 ft. 13 (2d8 + 4) Piercing damage.

Bonus Actions

Whelm. The sorrowlarch whelms one Medium or smaller creature it is grappling. While whelmed, the target has the Blinded and Restrained conditions, has Total Cover against attacks and other effects outside the sorrowlarch, and takes 6 (1d12) Acid damage at the start of each of the sorrowlarch's turns. The sorrowlarch can have only one creature whelmed at a time. If the sorrowlarch takes 12 damage or more in a single turn from the whelmed creature, the sorrowlarch must release the target at the end of that turn, and the target exits Prone in an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the sorrowlarch. If the sorrowlarch dies, a whelmed creature pulls free Prone by using 5 feet of movement.

Fixing Stare. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 13, one creature within 30 feet. Failure: Until the start of the sorrowlarch's next turn, the target has Disadvantage on attack rolls against creatures other than the sorrowlarch.

[concept] synthesis text: A hulking, sap-slathered giant of the deep caverns, its hide permanently glazed in a black tar that beads and drips from a lanky, spider-jointed frame built to scuttle up chimneys and sheer walls on long hooked limbs. It fights as an ambusher's brute, splashing caustic tar from its weeping joints to seal a foe's footing before dropping full weight and hammering with fists and knees; the stuff eats through hide and armor while it drags the slickened victim toward its perch. Half-remembering a sentinel's oath it can no longer name, it holds a meaningless stretch of tunnel with stubborn pride and will not retreat, fighting until its own bulk or the intruder gives out. It is crudely sapient (INT 6-8), and it is a proud sentinel bound to an ancient oath it no longer remembers.

audit finding below confidence gate: Immunities The block makes the drowned choir immune to Grappled, Prone, and Restrained, yet nothing in its own entries imposes those states and its Drowning Hands is pure melee, so a GM has no adjudication concern — but the immunity list combined with Incorporeal Movement and the hover fly speed creates a creature that cannot be grappled, proned, or restrained by any means, leaving no rules-legal counterplay to its mobility; this is a chassis-level concern, so flagged only as a self-consistency note that no entry creates these states.
audit finding below confidence gate: Incorporeal Movement The creature is a Large Undead with 178 HP and physical melee attacks, yet Incorporeal Movement frames it as bodiless while its Resitances and melee reach imply a physical form; the trait is not a bug by itself, but paired with the 'Drowned Choir' name and no other incorporeal-flavored ability, it reads as a type-fiction mismatch for a creature whose content otherwise expresses a corporeal drowned entity.
audit finding below confidence gate: Vitality Leech This is 2014-format save phrasing placement (save type and DC listed before the target and range), which the 2024 format writes as a saving throw with target and range stated in the standard order; the entry is adjudicable but the phrasing is format-inconsistent.
[reskin] audit rename: "Incorporeal Movement" -> "Ghostly Passage"
[reskin] audit rename: "Vitality Leech" -> "Dirge of the Drowned"
[reskin] 2014 conversion: one lint-gated call — both printings saved

```
Vampire
Medium undead, lawful evil
Armor Class

16 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

195 (23d8 + 92)

Speed: 

40 ft., climb 40 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


18 (+4)

	

18 (+4)

	

18 (+4)

	

17 (+3)

	

15 (+2)

	

18 (+4)

Skills: 

Perception +7, Stealth +9

Saving Throws: 

DEX +9, INT +8

Damage Resistances: 

necrotic

Damage Immunities: 

poison

Condition Immunities: 

exhaustion, poisoned

Senses: 

darkvision 120 ft., passive Perception 17

Languages: 

Common plus two other languages

Challenge

13 (10,000 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+5

Thornblood Garden. A creature that touches the vampire or hits it with a melee attack while within 5 feet of it takes 10 (3d6) necrotic damage.
Legendary Resistance. If the vampire fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.
Misty Escape. If the vampire drops to 0 hit points outside its resting place, the vampire uses Shape-Shift to become mist (no action required). If it can't use Shape-Shift, it is destroyed.
While it has 0 hit points in mist form, it can't return to its vampire form, and it must reach its resting place within 2 hours or be destroyed. Once in its resting place, it returns to its vampire form and is paralyzed until it regains any hit points, and it regains 1 hit point after spending 1 hour there.
Spider Climb. The vampire can climb difficult surfaces, including along ceilings, without needing to make an ability check.
Vampire Weakness. The vampire has these weaknesses: Forbiddance: the vampire can't enter a residence without an invitation from an occupant.
Running Water: the vampire takes 20 acid damage if it ends its turn in running water.
Stake to the Heart: If a weapon that deals piercing damage is driven into the vampire's heart while the vampire is incapacitated in its resting place, the vampire is paralyzed until the weapon is removed.
Sunlight: the vampire takes 20 radiant damage if it starts its turn in sunlight. While in sunlight, it has disadvantage on attack rolls and ability checks.
Shape-Shift. As a bonus action, if the vampire isn't in sunlight or running water, it shape-shifts into a Tiny bat (Speed 5 ft., fly speed 30 ft.) or a Medium cloud of mist (Speed 5 ft., fly speed 20 ft. [hover]), or it returns to its vampire form. Anything it is wearing transforms with it.
While in bat form, the vampire can't speak. Its game statistics, other than its size and Speed, are unchanged.
While in mist form, the vampire can't take any actions, speak, or manipulate objects. It is weightless and can enter an enemy's space and stop there. If air can pass through a space, the mist can do so, but it can't pass through liquid. It has resistance to all damage, except the damage it takes from sunlight.
Charm (Recharge 5-6). As a bonus action, the vampire casts charm person, requiring no spell components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 17), and the duration is 24 hours. The charmed target is a willing recipient of the vampire's Bite, the damage of which doesn't end the spell. When the spell ends, the target is unaware it was charmed by the vampire.

Actions
Multiattack: The vampire makes two Grave Strike attacks and uses Bite.
Grave Strike: Melee Weapon Attack: +9 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 8 (1d8 + 4) bludgeoning damage plus 7 (2d6) necrotic damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it is grappled (escape DC 17) by one of two hands.
Bite (Bat or Vampire Form Only): One creature within 5 feet that is willing or that is grappled, incapacitated, or restrained must make a DC 17 Constitution saving throw, taking 6 (1d4 + 4) piercing damage plus 13 (3d8) necrotic damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one. The target's hit point maximum decreases by an amount equal to the necrotic damage taken, and the vampire regains hit points equal to that amount. A Humanoid reduced to 0 hit points by this damage and then buried rises the following sunset as a vampire spawn under the vampire's control.
```

[concept] identity: A vampire who tends a walled garden of blood-fed thorns, it opens combat by slipping unseen through its own briars and burying a thorn-dagger in the neck of the nearest intruder, drinking deep before the body falls. Under pressure it does not flee its garden — it retreats only deeper, sacrificing lesser growths to herd prey apart and savoring the hunt as a gardener savors a slow harvest.

Traits

Hive Wound. A creature that touches the archon or hits it with a melee attack while within 5 feet of it takes 10 (3d6) Poison damage.

Aura of Authority. While in a 10-foot Emanation originating from the archon, the archon and its allies have Advantage on attack rolls and saving throws, provided the archon doesn't have the Incapacitated condition.

Actions

Multiattack. The archon makes three attacks, using Chitin Blade or Stinger Lunge in any combination.

Stinger Lunge. Melee Attack Roll: +10, reach 5 ft. 29 (7d6 + 5) Piercing damage.

Bonus Actions

Swarm Command (Recharge 5-6). Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 17, each creature of the archon's choice within 30 feet. Failure: The target immediately moves up to half its Speed in a direction of the archon's choice, using the safest available route.

Gargantuan Monstrosity, Unaligned

AC 18
Initiative -2 (8)

Skills Athletics +14, Perception +4

Senses blindsight 30 ft., tremorsense 60 ft.; Passive Perception 14

Languages None

CR 15 (XP 13,000; PB +5)

Traits

Tunneler. The purple worm can burrow through solid rock at half its Burrow Speed and leaves a 10-foot-diameter tunnel in its wake.

Actions

Multiattack. The purple worm makes one Bite attack and one Tail Stinger attack. It can replace one attack with a use of Engulfing Eruption.

Bite. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 22 (3d8 + 9) Piercing damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it has the Grappled condition (escape DC 22), and it has the Restrained condition until the grapple ends.

Tail Stinger. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 16 (2d6 + 9) Piercing damage plus 35 (10d6) Poison damage.

Engulfing Eruption. Dexterity Saving Throw: DC 21, one Large or smaller creature within 10 feet that the purple worm hit with a melee attack this turn. Failure: 42 (12d6) Piercing damage, and the target is swallowed. A swallowed creature has the Blinded and Restrained conditions, has Total Cover against attacks and other effects outside the purple worm, and takes 22 (5d8) Acid damage at the start of each of the purple worm's turns. If the purple worm takes 40 damage or more on a single turn from a creature inside it, it must succeed on a DC 21 Constitution saving throw at the end of that turn or regurgitate all swallowed creatures, each of which falls in a space within 10 feet of the purple worm and has the Prone condition. If the purple worm dies, a swallowed creature is no longer Restrained and can escape from the corpse using 15 feet of movement, exiting with the Prone condition.

Bonus Actions

Swallow. Strength Saving Throw: DC 21, one Large or smaller creature Grappled by the purple worm (it can have up to three creatures swallowed at a time). Failure: The target is swallowed by the purple worm, and the Grappled condition ends. A swallowed creature has the Blinded and Restrained conditions, has Total Cover against attacks and other effects outside the purple worm, and takes 17 (5d6) Acid damage at the start of each of the purple worm's turns.
If the purple worm takes 30 damage or more on a single turn from a creature inside it, the purple worm must succeed on a DC 21 Constitution saving throw at the end of that turn or regurgitate all swallowed creatures, each of which falls in a space within 5 feet of the purple worm and has the Prone condition. If the purple worm dies, any swallowed creature no longer has the Restrained condition and can escape from the corpse using 20 feet of movement, exiting Prone.

[concept] identity: The Purple Worm opens combat by erupting from the ground beneath the densest cluster of foes, its maw gaping to swallow a champion whole — helm, mail, and all — then coils its armoured hide around the survivors to crush them against the blades and pauldrons grinding in its gut. Each round it alternates between biting to seize a new victim and dragging one from its gullet to feast, its hide ringing with war-band steel; when bloodied it does not flee but throws its full coils into the largest target, fighting to the death to gorge further. It toys neither with intent nor cunning — it hunts like a thing of pure appetite, and the armoury swallowed within rattles and shifts against its hide with every movement.

Pit Fiend

Large Fiend, Lawful Evil

AC 21
Initiative +2 (12)

HP 337 (27d10 + 189)

Speed 30 ft., Fly 60 ft.

Resistances Cold

Immunities Fire, Poison; Poisoned

Senses darkvision 120 ft.; Passive Perception 20

Languages Infernal; telepathy 120 ft.

CR 20 (XP 25,000; PB +6)

Traits

Oath Collector. When a creature within 30 feet of the pit fiend drops to 0 Hit Points, the pit fiend can choose to collect the creature's dying oath, gaining a fragment of its resolve; it may immediately expend a Legendary Action use to take the Dodge action as it savors the vow.

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the pit fiend fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.

Diabolical Restoration. If the pit fiend dies outside the Nine Hells, its body disappears in sulfurous smoke, and it gains a new body instantly, reviving with all its Hit Points somewhere in the Nine Hells.

Fear Aura. The pit fiend emanates an aura in a 20-foot Emanation while it doesn't have the Incapacitated condition. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 21, any enemy that starts its turn in the aura. Failure: The target has the Frightened condition until the start of its next turn. Success: The target is immune to this pit fiend's aura for 24 hours.

Magic Resistance. The pit fiend has Advantage on saving throws against spells and other magical effects.

Actions

Multiattack. The pit fiend makes one Bite attack, two Devilish Claw attacks, and one Fiery Mace attack.

Bite. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 18 (3d6 + 8) Piercing damage. If the target is a creature, it must make the following saving throw. Constitution Saving Throw: DC 21. Failure: The target has the Poisoned condition. While Poisoned, the target can't regain Hit Points and takes 21 (6d6) Poison damage at the start of each of its turns, and it repeats the save at the end of each of its turns, ending the effect on itself on a success. After 1 minute, it succeeds automatically.

Fiery Mace. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 22 (4d6 + 8) Force damage plus 21 (6d6) Fire damage.

Devilish Claw. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 26 (4d8 + 8) Necrotic damage.

Hellfire Spellcasting (Recharge 4-6). The pit fiend casts Fireball (level 5 version) twice, requiring no Material components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 21). It can replace one Fireball with Hold Monster (level 7 version) or Wall of Fire.

[concept] identity: The Pit Fiend opens by unfurling a banner of scorched iron and demanding the field's surrender, and any dying knight who answers with an oath finds that oath branded into its own hide as a binding it can spend. Each round it fights beside its sworn retinue, using its parry and command aura to shoulder blows meant for its troops while it converts every fresh vow into a burst of punishing flame. It is vain, patient, and utterly certain of its authority — it will not flee a battle it believes it is winning, and it toys with broken foes to coax one last promise out of them.

Bone Sun Archlich
Medium undead, neutral evil
Armor Class

20 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

315 (42d8 + 126)

Speed: 

30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


11 (+0)

	

16 (+3)

	

16 (+3)

	

21 (+5)

	

14 (+2)

	

16 (+3)

Arcana +17, History +11, Insight +8, Perception +8

Saving Throws: 

CON +9, INT +11, WIS +8

Damage Resistances: 

cold, lightning

Damage Immunities: 

necrotic, poison

Condition Immunities: 

charmed, exhaustion, frightened, paralyzed, poisoned

darkvision 120 ft., passive Perception 18

All

Challenge

20 (25,000 XP)

+6

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the bone sun archlich fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.
Spirit Jar. If destroyed, the bone sun archlich reforms in 1d10 days if it has a spirit jar, reviving with all its hit points. The new body appears in an unoccupied space within the bone sun archlich's lair.
Protective Magic (3/Day) (Reaction). When the bone sun archlich is hit by an attack, it casts shield in response, gaining a +5 bonus to AC until the start of its next turn, including against the triggering attack.

Actions
Multiattack: The bone sun archlich makes three attacks, using Eldritch Burst or Paralyzing Touch. It can replace one attack with a use of Spellcasting to cast Blindness/Deafness.
Eldritch Burst: Melee or Ranged Spell Attack: +11 to hit, reach 5 ft. or range 120 ft., one target. Hit: 31 (4d12 + 5) force damage.
Paralyzing Touch: Melee Spell Attack: +11 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 15 (3d6 + 5) cold damage, and the target is paralyzed until the start of the bone sun archlich's next turn.
Spellcasting: The bone sun archlich casts one of the following spells, requiring no material components and using Intelligence as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 19): -

At will: detect magic, detect thoughts, dispel magic, fireball, invisibility, lightning bolt, mage hand, prestidigitation

2/day each: animate dead, dimension door, plane shift

1/day each: chain lightning, finger of death, power word kill, scrying
Legendary Actions

The bone sun archlich can take 3 legendary actions, choosing from the options below. Only one legendary action option can be used at a time and only at the end of another creature's turn. The bone sun archlich regains spent legendary actions at the start of its turn.

Deathly Teleport: The bone sun archlich teleports up to 60 feet to an unoccupied space it can see, and each creature within 10 feet of the space it left takes 11 (2d10) necrotic damage.
Disrupt Life: Each creature that isn't an undead within 20 feet of the bone sun archlich must make a DC 19 Constitution saving throw, taking 31 (9d6) necrotic damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one. The bone sun archlich can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
Frightening Gaze: The bone sun archlich casts fear, using the same spellcasting ability as Spellcasting. The bone sun archlich can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
```

Ancient Gold Dragon
Gargantuan dragon, lawful good
Armor Class

22 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

546 (28d20 + 252)

Speed: 

40 ft., fly 80 ft., swim 40 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


30 (+10)

	

14 (+2)

	

29 (+9)

	

18 (+4)

	

17 (+3)

	

28 (+9)

Skills: 

Insight +10, Perception +17, Persuasion +16, Stealth +9

Saving Throws: 

DEX +9, WIS +10

Damage Immunities: 

fire

Senses: 

blindsight 60 ft., darkvision 120 ft., passive Perception 27

Languages: 

Common, Draconic

Challenge

24 (62,000 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+7

Legendary Resistance (4/Day). If the gold dragon fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.
Amphibious. The gold dragon can breathe air and water.

Actions
Multiattack: The gold dragon makes three Rend attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of (A) Spellcasting to cast Guiding Bolt (level 4 version) or (B) Weakening Breath.
Rend: Melee Weapon Attack: +17 to hit, reach 15 ft., one target. Hit: 19 (2d8 + 10) slashing damage plus 9 (2d8) fire damage.
Spellcasting: The gold dragon casts one of the following spells, requiring no material components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 24, +16 to hit with spell attacks): -

At will: detect magic, guiding bolt, shapechange

1/day each: flame strike, word of recall, zone of truth
Fire Breath (Recharge 5-6): Each creature in a 90-foot cone must make a DC 24 Dexterity saving throw, taking 71 (13d10) fire damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one.
Weakening Breath: Each creature that isn't currently affected by this breath in a 90-foot cone must make a DC 24 Strength saving throw. On a failed save, the target has disadvantage on Strength-based D20 tests and subtracts 5 (1d10) from its damage rolls. It repeats the save at the end of each of its turns, ending the effect on itself on a success. After 1 minute, it succeeds automatically.
Legendary Actions

The gold dragon can take 3 legendary actions, choosing from the options below. Only one legendary action option can be used at a time and only at the end of another creature's turn. The gold dragon regains spent legendary actions at the start of its turn.

Banish: One creature the gold dragon can see within 120 feet of it must make a DC 24 Charisma saving throw. On a failed save, the target takes 24 (7d6) force damage, and it is incapacitated and transported to a harmless demiplane until the start of the gold dragon's next turn, at which point it reappears in an unoccupied space of the gold dragon's choice within 120 feet of the gold dragon. Whether it succeeds or fails, the gold dragon can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
Guiding Light: The gold dragon uses Spellcasting to cast guiding bolt (cast at 4th level).
Pounce: The gold dragon moves up to half its speed, and it makes one Rend attack.
```

Senses Passive Perception 13

Languages Common, Goblin

CR 6 (XP 2,300; PB +3)

Traits

Guarded Stance. While Vashka isn't Bloodied, melee attack rolls against it are made with Disadvantage.

Pack Tactics. The hobgoblin has Advantage on an attack roll against a creature if at least one of the hobgoblin's allies is within 5 feet of the creature and the ally doesn't have the Incapacitated condition.

Actions

Multiattack. Vashka makes two attacks, using Longsword or Longbow in any combination.

Longsword. Melee Attack Roll: +7, reach 5 ft. 22 (4d8 + 4) Slashing damage.

Longbow. Ranged Attack Roll: +7, range 150/600 ft. 22 (4d8 + 4) Piercing damage.

Reactions

Riposte. Trigger: A creature within 5 feet of Vashka misses it with a melee attack roll. Response: Vashka makes one Longsword attack against that creature.

Large Monstrosity, Unaligned

AC 15
Initiative -1 (9)

Languages None

CR 9 (XP 5,000; PB +4)

Traits

Two-Armed Pin. While the butcher is grappling a creature, the butcher fights with only two of its scythe-arms, and the Grappled creature has Disadvantage on ability checks made to escape the grapple.

Seam-Shed Acid. A creature that touches the butcher or hits it with a melee attack while within 5 feet of it takes 7 (2d6) Acid damage.

Actions

Multiattack. The butcher makes two attacks, using Spitting Maw or Scythe-Arm in any combination.

Spitting Maw. Melee Attack Roll: +8, reach 10 ft. 31 (6d8 + 4) Piercing damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it has the Grappled condition (escape DC 16).

Caustic Spew (Recharge 5-6). Constitution Saving Throw: DC 15, each creature in a 30-foot Cone. Failure: 32 (5d12) Acid damage, and the target has the Poisoned condition until the end of its next turn. Success: Half damage only.

Scythe-Arm. Melee Attack Roll: +8, reach 5 ft. 31 (6d8 + 4) Slashing damage.

Bonus Actions

Smother. The butcher smothers one Medium or smaller creature it is grappling. While smothered, the target has the Blinded and Restrained conditions, has Total Cover against attacks and other effects outside the butcher, and takes 10 (3d6) Acid damage at the start of each of the butcher's turns. The butcher can have only one creature smothered at a time. If the butcher takes 25 damage or more on a single turn from the smothered creature, the butcher must release the target at the end of that turn, and the target exits Prone in an unoccupied space within 10 feet of the butcher. If the butcher dies, a smothered creature pulls free Prone by using 10 feet of movement.

Actions

Multiattack. The drowned makes two Brine-Coil attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Vitality Leech.

Brine-Coil. Melee Attack Roll: +6 (with Advantage if the target is Grappled by the drowned), reach 5 ft. 12 (2d8 + 3) Bludgeoning damage plus 7 (2d6) Acid damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it has the Grappled condition (escape DC 14).

Vitality Leech. Constitution Saving Throw: DC 14, one creature within 30 feet. Failure: 18 (4d8) Acid damage, and the drowned regains Hit Points equal to half the damage dealt. Success: Half damage only.

Bonus Actions

False Form. The drowned shape-shifts to resemble an ordinary object or fixture of the place it waits in while retaining its game statistics, or it returns to its true form. While it is motionless in its false form, it is indistinguishable from that feature.

[concept] identity: The thing in the well waits at the bottom of a shaft of black water, folded into the shape of a drowned cur or a child's rag doll, and does not move until a face leans over the rim — then it unfurls its brine-slick bulk up the stonework, swallowing the light with it so the well's mouth goes dark. Every round after the first is spent hauling itself one coil further up the shaft, wrapping the masonry with adhesive brine until the walls themselves are its hands. Bloodied, it does not flee; it lets go of the wall and drops, dragging whatever it has grabbed down into the black water with it, holding the prey under until it stops struggling and can toy with the body at leisure.
[concept] lore: A thing that fell into the old well long ago and grew into the shape of whatever it first drowned, it wants only to be fed without ever being seen, and it has learned the patience of water.

```
Hask the Scarred
Medium humanoid, neutral
Armor Class

16 (studded leather, shield)

Hit Points: 

112 (15d8 + 45)

30 ft.

passive Perception 11

+3

Riposte (Reaction). When a creature within 5 feet of Hask misses it with a melee attack, Hask makes one Shortsword attack against that creature.

Actions
Multiattack: Hask makes three Shortsword attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Shield Bash.
Shortsword: Melee Weapon Attack: +7 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 11 (2d6 + 4) piercing damage, and Hask has advantage on its next attack roll against the target before the end of its next turn.
Shield Bash: One creature within 5 feet that Hask can see must succeed on a DC 15 Strength saving throw or take 9 (2d4 + 4) bludgeoning damage. If the target is a Medium or smaller creature, it is knocked prone.
